SockShop = BlueprintApplication() {
  cart_ctr = LinuxContainer(cart_db.dial_addr, cart_service.grpc.bind_addr) {
    cart_proc = GolangProcessNode(cart_db.dial_addr, cart_service.grpc.bind_addr) {
      cart_db.client = MongoClient(cart_db.dial_addr)
      cart_proc.logger = SLogger()
      cart_proc.stdoutmetriccollector = StdoutMetricCollector()
      cart_service = CartService(cart_db.client)
      cart_service.grpc_server = GRPCServer(cart_service, cart_service.grpc.bind_addr)
    }
  }
  cart_db.addr
  cart_db.bind_addr = AddressConfig()
  cart_db.ctr = MongoDBProcess(cart_db.bind_addr)
  cart_db.dial_addr = AddressConfig()
  cart_service.grpc.addr
  cart_service.grpc.bind_addr = AddressConfig()
  cart_service.grpc.dial_addr = AddressConfig()
  cart_service.handler.visibility
  catalogue_ctr = LinuxContainer(catalogue_db.dial_addr, catalogue_service.grpc.bind_addr) {
    catalogue_proc = GolangProcessNode(catalogue_db.dial_addr, catalogue_service.grpc.bind_addr) {
      catalogue_db.client = MySqlClient(catalogue_db.dial_addr)
      catalogue_proc.logger = SLogger()
      catalogue_proc.stdoutmetriccollector = StdoutMetricCollector()
      catalogue_service = CatalogueService(catalogue_db.client)
      catalogue_service.grpc_server = GRPCServer(catalogue_service, catalogue_service.grpc.bind_addr)
    }
  }
  catalogue_db.addr
  catalogue_db.bind_addr = AddressConfig()
  catalogue_db.ctr = MySqlDBContainer(catalogue_db.bind_addr)
  catalogue_db.dial_addr = AddressConfig()
  catalogue_service.grpc.addr
  catalogue_service.grpc.bind_addr = AddressConfig()
  catalogue_service.grpc.dial_addr = AddressConfig()
  catalogue_service.handler.visibility
  frontend.handler.visibility
  frontend.http.addr
  frontend.http.bind_addr = AddressConfig()
  frontend.http.dial_addr = AddressConfig()
  frontend_ctr = LinuxContainer(cart_service.grpc.dial_addr, catalogue_service.grpc.dial_addr, frontend.http.bind_addr, order_service.grpc.dial_addr, user_service.grpc.dial_addr) {
    frontend_proc = GolangProcessNode(cart_service.grpc.dial_addr, catalogue_service.grpc.dial_addr, frontend.http.bind_addr, order_service.grpc.dial_addr, user_service.grpc.dial_addr) {
      cart_service.client = cart_service.clientpool
      cart_service.clientpool = ClientPool(cart_service.grpc_client, 10) {
        cart_service.grpc_client = GRPCClient(cart_service.grpc.dial_addr)
      }
      catalogue_service.client = catalogue_service.clientpool
      catalogue_service.clientpool = ClientPool(catalogue_service.grpc_client, 10) {
        catalogue_service.grpc_client = GRPCClient(catalogue_service.grpc.dial_addr)
      }
      frontend = Frontend(user_service.client, catalogue_service.client, cart_service.client, order_service.client)
      frontend.http_server = HTTPServer(frontend, frontend.http.bind_addr)
      frontend_proc.logger = SLogger()
      frontend_proc.stdoutmetriccollector = StdoutMetricCollector()
      order_service.client = order_service.clientpool
      order_service.clientpool = ClientPool(order_service.grpc_client, 10) {
        order_service.grpc_client = GRPCClient(order_service.grpc.dial_addr)
      }
      user_service.client = user_service.clientpool
      user_service.clientpool = ClientPool(user_service.grpc_client, 10) {
        user_service.grpc_client = GRPCClient(user_service.grpc.dial_addr)
      }
